padding-top padding-bottom"> <p class="svelte-158dia6"> <div class="multiline-clamp svelte-1r39353" style="--mc-lineClamp: var(--defaultClampOverride, 5);" role="text"> <span class="multiline-clamp__text svelte-1r39353"><span dir="auto" class="svelte-158dia6"><!-- HTML_TAG_START -->PROPFINDER — PLAYER PROP RESEARCH MADE EASY
 
PropFinder turns the prop market into a research lab. Built for MLB, NFL, NBA, NHL, WNBA, and NCAA bettors who want to know why a line is off — not just hear someone say it is.

Here's what's inside:
 - A free preview so you can test the app before you even purchase
 - Thousands of pre-game props ranked by value
 - Cheatsheets that summarize the slate in seconds
 - Side-by-side odds from every major sportsbook and DFS PickEm site
 - Player history charts, gamelogs, and streak tracking
 - MLB-specific tools: HR Cheatsheet, Statcast exit velocity, ballpark + weather data
 - Daily injury feeds
 - A clean way to save props and send them straight to your sportsbook
 - Visualizations that actually mean something

Casual fan or full-time grinder, PropFinder is built so the answers come to you — not the other way around.

START FOR FREE
No card. No trial countdown. Open the app and start digging. Upgrade only when you've decided we're worth it.

SEARCH LIKE AN ANALYST
Type a player. Filter by matchup, line, book, or trend. Sort by edge. Whatever question you have about a prop, PropFinder has a screen for it.

CHEATSHEETS THAT RESPECT YOUR TIME
Don't have an hour to grind before lock? Our cheatsheets do the heavy lifting for you.

Every cheatsheet pulls every piece of research you'd normally chase across five different tabs — recent form, hit rates over the last 5, 10, and 20 games, matchup splits, opponent rank, home/away breakdowns, line movement, and the best available odds — and stacks it all into one scrollable screen. Sort by any column. Filter to the props you care about. Color coding makes the standouts impossible to miss.

It's the entire slate, distilled. MLB, NFL, NBA, WNBA, NHL, and NCAA — refreshed daily, ready when you open the app.

STATS YOU CAN ACTUALLY READ
A spreadsheet doesn't help if you can't see the story. PropFinder draws it. Recent form, matchup splits, hit rates, home/away breakdowns — all visual, all instant, all on one screen.

ODDS, ALL IN ONE PLACE
Every book. Every line. One view. Stop flipping between apps to chase a half-point — we surface the best number the moment it appears.

SAVE PROPS. SEND THEM STRAIGHT TO YOUR BOOK.
See a prop you like? Tap to save it. Your saved props build a personal shortlist as you research, so nothing gets lost in the shuffle.

Done researching? You can export straights or parlays directly to your favorite sports books. 

BUILT FOR BASEBALL
MLB is where PropFinder started, and it shows. Pitch-level exit velocity filters. Park factors. Weather. Pitcher vs. lineup splits. Tools you won't find bundled anywhere else.

PREMIUM ACCESS
Research props free. Optional Premium access unlocks the full PropFinder toolkit across supported leagues, including MLB, NFL, NBA, WNBA, NHL, and NCAA.

Premium availability, features, and plan details are shown in PropFinder when you choose to upgrade.

RESPONSIBLE GAMBLING

WHO BUILDS PROPFINDER?
Engineers, modelers, and bettors. We got tired of bouncing between tabs and spreadsheets to research a single prop, so we built the app we wished existed. Every feature ships because one of us needed it on a real slate.

DOWNLOAD PROPFINDER
Smarter research. Cleaner data. More importantly, once you look under the hood, all this app really does is take publicly available data from FanGraphs and MLB’s Baseball Savant and repackage it into color-coded charts. That’s it. Everything in here is already free on those sites, presented by the actual sources so you know the information is correct, unlike the info in PropFinder. There’s nothing original. It’s not the worst app I’ve seen, but there’s no reason to pay for it when the free data already exists in other places. I won’t be using it again. is slow to load and most features aren’t even working for me. Everything in here is already free on those sites, presented by the actual sources so you know the information is correct, unlike the info in PropFinder. There’s nothing original. No unique analysis or anything. It’s just a subscription fee slapped on top of free information. It’s not the worst app I’ve seen, but there’s no reason to pay for it when the free data already exists in other places. I won’t be using it again. No piecing it together across five sites.\n\nTRENDS AND HIT RATES\nSee which players have been hitting their lines and which runs are built on thin ground.
